# ABNeon 72-hour post-close study

Production cohort rows: 144; actual closed_at rows: 98; reclassified rows without actual closed_at: 46.
Era assignment by detected_at: 4H before 2026-11-14, 1D from 2026-11-14: 4H=118; 1D=26.
After-close classification counts: {"wrong_exit_plus10_within_observed_window":11,"right_exit_no_plus10_in_72h":68,"pending_72h_not_mature":19,"no_actual_closed_at":46,"no_post_close_price_bars":0}.
CoinGlass actual requests including retries: 286; by endpoint: {"oi15":171,"fut15":33,"spot15":30,"fut1h":26,"spot1h":26}; 429 responses=1; 5-minute pauses=1.
Good-entry paths: 8357 observed rows for 19/19 IDs; window entry−24h through min(entry+96h, now). Completed 96h paths=16; incomplete because 96h has not elapsed=3.
Completed-path coverage exceptions: 3794 BARD-USDT: 479/480 observed 15m candles; 3824 ARIA-USDT: 479/480 observed 15m candles; 3838 PONS-USDT: 479/480 observed 15m candles; 3898 STO-USDT: 479/480 observed 15m candles; no missing candles were fabricated.
Still-incomplete paths: 3953 MMT-USDT: 275 candles, last=2026-11-19T11:15:00.000Z; 3958 PIEVERSE-USDT: 237 candles, last=2026-11-19T11:15:00.000Z; 3963 EVAA-USDT: 169 candles, last=2026-11-19T11:15:00.000Z.
Exit price window starts at the first complete 15m candle opening at/after closed_at, ends at min(closed_at+72h, now); threshold checks use candle highs. Samples without a full 72h and no +10 touch are pending, not called right.
Database timestamps without an explicit zone are treated as UTC and emitted as ISO-8601 Z. No updated_at value is substituted for a missing closed_at.
The exit metrics sample uses the last fully closed 15m candle before closed_at; entry metrics are sampled using the same 15m bar mapping as the prior t0.csv study. `total_vs_avg20/120_pct` means percent of the respective average (100=1.0×).
RECLASSIFIED rows with null closed_at have no verified market-exit timestamp and are retained in events.csv/after72h.csv as unclassifiable; updated_at is not substituted for close time.
